Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any informatics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 326. The projective resolution has length at most 775 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to informatics theory, we decompose the problem into 326 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 775. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
